## Friday, August 28, 2026 Control-172 strict read-only control + daytrading cron dispatch fix (VERIFIED)

Control-172 at ~17:01 UTC Aug 28 (market OPEN). Strict GET-only. 8 endpoints 200, 3 404 (expected). HEALTHY code/deploy (2.6.0), DEGRADED external. **Critical fix deployed AND live-verified:** Cloudflare stopped dispatching the daytrading cron on Aug 28. Both `*/5 13-21 * * 1-5` and `*/5 * * * 1-5` were registered in the schedules API but never dispatched (zero daytrading runs for 19+ hours). Root cause: Cloudflare platform issue with day-of-week restrictions (`1-5`) in cron triggers — working schedules (`*/10 * * * *`, `7-59/30 * * * *`) both use `*` for DOW. Fix: `1-59/5 * * * *` (every 5 min at :01/:06/:11/:16/:21/:26/:31/:36/:41/:46/:51/:56, offset to avoid `*/10` overlap, `*` for DOW). Internal MARKET_CLOSED check gates to market hours. **VERIFIED: 3+ consecutive daytrading_cron runs (4139 at 17:17, 4141 at 17:22). SWING_OWNED_EXCLUDE first exercise (COIN skipped as swing-owned). MSTR BUY 7 @ $127.25 filled (trade 741).** Deployed via direct CF API PUT (HTTP 200, deployment_id 348c28999d87463c80f77edcfa045ab5) with `crons` field in metadata JSON. Schedules confirmed via CF API. 224 tests / 845 assertions, typecheck clean, diff clean. Reliability-only: no caps, trading behavior, or risk logic changed. Caps 5000/3700/2000 USD unchanged. Code 79583d8. Four schedules: `1-59/5 * * * *`, `0 22 * * 1-5`, `7-59/30 * * * *`, `*/10 * * * *`. Docs HEAD: this commit (local only, push BLOCKED: github_pat not in vault).

**Deploy lesson:** Including `crons` in the PUT /scripts metadata is the authoritative way to register cron schedules. The separate PUT /schedules endpoint registers the schedule in the API but Cloudflare's dispatch system may not pick it up. Always include `crons` in the deploy metadata.

**DEPLOY LESSON REINFORCED:** Control-103's typecheck "fixes" introduced a new regression by renaming a parameter without updating all references. Always run `bun run typecheck` AND verify it exits clean (no output) after any type-related change, not just rely on tests passing.

Missing daytrading cron runs (unlogged) interleaved with CYCLE_LEASE_HELD streaks (3551/3555/3556 daytrading, 3554/3557 maintenance). Confirms "silent throw before lease release": a cron invocation hits Cloudflare free-tier subrequest/CPU limits, throws before logging or releasing lease, subsequent invocations log CYCLE_LEASE_HELD until 10-min TTL expires, then another silent throw repeats the cycle. Run 3524 (17:36 UTC) caught an explicit "Too many subrequests by single Worker invocation" error; the others threw silently with no run_log row at all. The daytrading lease is strategy-isolated so tonight's 22:00 UTC swing run (separate swing lease) is unaffected. Root cause is external resource constraint (Cloudflare Workers Free subrequest/CPU limits), not a code defect. The approved paid-plan upgrade is the remedy; no deploy is warranted from this control.

Status: HEALTHY (code/deployment), DEGRADED (external data-feed/resource + run-log delivery observability gaps + CYCLE_LEASE_HELD streaks from silent subrequest/CPU exhaustion throws). Tonight 22:00 UTC swing run = first natural swing execution on deployed 2.6.0 with empty swing book.
